[39m
[31m+         }

[31m+     };

[31m+     const

{
[39m
[31m+         if (!user)

[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             const

[39m
[31m+

[39m
[31m+                    
 'Authorization': `Bearer

[39m
[31m+                 }

[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             if

[39m
[31m+

[31m+

[31m+

[31m+             }

[31m+         } catch

[31m+

[31m+         }

[31m+     };

[39m
[31m+     return (
[39m
[31m+

[31m+             
{children}

[31m+     return context;

[31m+ export function 
filterLibraryQuizzes(
[39m
[31m+     quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[],
[39m
[31m+     query: string,
[39m
[31m+     subject: string
[39m
[31m+ ): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     let result = 
quizzes;
[39m
[31m+     if 
(query.trim()) {
[39m
[31m+         const 
lower = 
query.toLowerCase();
[39m
[31m+         result = 
result.filter(
[39m
[31m+             q => q.
title.toLowerCase().includ
es(lower) || q.subject.toL
owerCase().includes(lower)
[39m
[31m+         );
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (subject 
!== 'all') {
[39m
[31m+         result = 
result.filter(q => 
q.subject === subject);
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return result;
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

 subject: 
noteForm.subject.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                    
 content: 
noteForm.content.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                    
 linkedQuizId: noteForm.li
nkedQuizId.trim() || null,
[39m
[31m+                 }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             setNote
Form(EMPTY_NOTE_FORM);
[39m
[31m+             
setNoteFormErrors({});
[39m
[31m+             
setShowNoteForm(false);
[39m
[31m+             
refresh();
[39m
[31m+         } catch 
(err) {
[39m
[31m+             
console.error('Failed to 
save note:', err);
[39m
[31m+         } finally {
[39m
[31m+             
setNoteSubmitting(false);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     }

[39m
[31m+     async function 
handlePublishToggle(note: 
Note) {
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             await a
piFetch(`/api/notes/${note
.id || (note as 
any)._id}`, {
[39m
[31m+                 
method: 'PATCH',
[39m
[31m+                 
body: { published: 
!note.published }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             
refresh();
[39m
[31m+         } catch 
(err) {
[39m
[31m+             
console.error('Failed to 
toggle publish state:', 
err);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+

[31m+ // Pure filter

[39m
[31m+ export function 
filterLibraryQuizzes(
[39m
[31m+     quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[],
[39m
[31m+     query: string,
[39m
[31m+     subject: string
[39m
[31m+ ): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     let result = 
quizzes;
[39m
[31m+     if 
(query.trim()) {
[39m
[31m+         const 
lower = 
query.toLowerCase();
[39m
[31m+         result = 
result.filter(
[39m
[31m+             q => q.
title.toLowerCase().includ
es(lower) || q.subject.toL

[39m
[31m+         );
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (subject 
!== 'all') {
[39m
[31m+         result = 
result.filter(q => 
q.subject === subject);
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return result;
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[31m+     async function 
handleNoteSubmit(e: 
React.FormEvent) {
[39m
[31m+         
e.preventDefault();
[39m
[31m+         const 
errors: { title?: string;

[39m
[31m+         if 
(!noteForm.title.trim())

[39m
[31m+         if (!noteFo
rm.content.trim()) 
errors.content = 'Content 
is required';
[39m
[31m+

[39m
[31m+         if (Object.
keys(errors).length > 0)

[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+

[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             await

[39m
[31m+

[39m
[31m+

[39m
[31m+                    
 title: 
noteForm.title.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                    
 subject: 
noteForm.subject.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                    
 content: 
noteForm.content.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                    
 linkedQuizId: noteForm.li
nkedQuizId.trim() || null,
[39m
[31m+                 }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             setNote
Form(EMPTY_NOTE_FORM);
[39m
[31m+             
setNoteFormErrors({});
[39m
[31m+             
setShowNoteForm(false);
[39m
[31m+             
refresh();
[39m
[31m+         } catch 
(err) {
[39m
[31m+             
console.error('Failed to 
save note:', err);
[39m
[31m+         } finally {
[39m
[31m+             
setNoteSubmitting(false);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[31m+ //

eact-refresh/only-export-c

[39m
[31m+ export function 
filterLibraryQuizzes(
[39m
[31m+     quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[],
[39m
[31m+     query: string,
[39m
[31m+     subject: string
[39m
[31m+ ): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     let result = 
quizzes;
[39m
[31m+     if 
(query.trim()) {
[39m
[31m+         const 
lower = 
query.toLowerCase();
[39m
[31m+         result = 
result.filter(
[39m
[31m+             q => q.
title.toLowerCase().includ
es(lower) || q.subject.toL
owerCase().includes(lower)
[39m
[31m+         );
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (subject 
!== 'all') {
[39m
[31m+         result = 
result.filter(q => 
q.subject === subject);
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return result;
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function 
sortQuizzes(quizzes: 
LibraryQuiz[], sort: 
string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return 
[...quizzes].sort((a, b) 
=> {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'plays') return 
(b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays 
?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort 
=== 'rating') return 
(b.rating ?? 0) - 
(a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ interface 
QuizCardProps {
[39m
[31m+     quiz: 
LibraryQuiz;
[39m
[31m+     index: number;
[39m
[31m+     subjectStyle: 
{ bg: string; text: 
string; dot: string };
[39m
[31m+     diffStyle: { 
bg: string; text: string 
} | null;
[39m
[31m+     onPreview?: () 
=> void;
[39m
[31m+ }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ function 
QuizCard({ quiz, index, 
subjectStyle, diffStyle, 
onPreview }: 
QuizCardProps) {
[39m
[31m+     const 
[hovered, setHovered] = 
useState(false);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     return (
[39m
[31m+         <motion.div
[39m
[31m+             
initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 
16 }}
[39m
[31m+             
animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 
0 }}
[39m
[31m+             
transition={{ delay: 
index * 0.05 }}
[39m
[31m+             
onMouseEnter={() => 
setHovered(true)}
[39m

[31m+ function

[39m
[31m+     if (code.includ
es('invalid-credential')

ssword') || code.includes(

[31m+         return

[39m
[31m+     }
[39m

es('too-many-requests')) {
[39m
[31m+         return

[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includ
es('user-disabled')) {
[39m
[31m+         return

[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includ

[39m
[31m+         return

[39m
[31m+     }

[39m
[31m+         return

[39m
[31m+     }

[31m+         return

[31m+     }

[31m+ function

string {
[39m

(iso).toLocaleDateString(u
ndefined, { month:
